\begin{titlingpage}
\vspace*{3cm}
\begin{center}

{\large\itshape Modular Homotopy Theory for Algebraic Factorization Algebras on Algebraic Curves}

\vspace{0.8cm}

{\normalsize Volume~2}

\vspace{1.5cm}

{\HUGE\scshape Holomorphic--Topological QFT}

\vspace{1.5cm}

{\Large\itshape $A_\infty$ Chiral Algebras and\\
Three-Dimensional Quantum Gravity}

\vspace{3cm}

{\large Raeez Lorgat}

\vspace{2cm}

\end{center}
\end{titlingpage}

\begin{abstract}
\itshape

\noindent
Let $\cA$ be a chirally Koszul chiral algebra on a smooth
algebraic curve~$C$.  Its chiral derived centre is the derived
endomorphism object of $\cA$ in the chiral $\cA$--$\cA$-bimodule
category on the Ran space,
\[
\cZ^{\mathrm{der}}_{\mathrm{ch}}(\cA)
:=\mathrm{REnd}^{\mathrm{ch}}_{\cA\text{-}\cA}(\cA)
\simeq C^\bullet_{\mathrm{ch}}(\cA,\cA),
\]
where the last identification uses the chiral bar resolution on the
Koszul locus.  The pair
$(\cZ^{\mathrm{der}}_{\mathrm{ch}}(\cA),\cA)$ carries two
factorisation structures at once: holomorphic factorisation at
coincident points on~$C$ (the closed colour), and topological
factorisation along a transverse real direction~$\R$ (the open
colour). The directed two-coloured
Swiss-cheese-chiral-topological dioperad $\SCchtop$ is the
algebraic record of this pairing.  A physical 3d
holomorphic--topological quantum field theory on $C\times\R$
requires, in addition, BV/HT realisation data comparing this algebraic
pair with observables.

\smallskip

\noindent
\textbf{Master theorem}
(Theorem~\ref{thm:universal-holography-master}).
The assignment
\[
 \Phi_{\mathrm{hol}}\colon
 \mathrm{HTReal}^{\mathrm{Kosz},\omega,\mathrm{BL},\Xi}_{X}
 \longrightarrow
 \mathrm{HT\text{-}QFT}_{X \times \R},
 \qquad
 (\cA,\omega,\mathrm{BL},\Xi) \longmapsto T_{\cA,\Xi},
\]
(domain: chirally Koszul chiral algebras on a curve $X$ with
non-critical conformal vector $\omega$, boundary linearisation BL,
and HT/BV realisation datum $\Xi$; target: 3d
holomorphic--topological QFTs on $X \times \R$)
is a realisation functor sending
$(\cA,\omega,\mathrm{BL},\Xi)$ to a 3d holomorphic--topological
gauge theory $T_{\cA,\Xi}$ with
$\mathrm{Obs}^\partial(T_{\cA,\Xi}) \simeq \cA$ and
$\mathrm{Obs}^{\mathrm{bulk}}(T_{\cA,\Xi}) \simeq
\cZ^{\mathrm{der}}_{\mathrm{ch}}(\cA)$ in the declared comparison
ambient.  The pair is an $\SCchtop$-algebra; the closed colour
promotes to an $E_3^{\mathrm{top}}$ factorisation algebra only after
the holomorphic translations are made $Q$-exact by the specified
antighost/topologisation datum.  The equivalence holds on the original
chain complex for finite-propagation strata and in the
weight-completed Banach ambient (Kontsevich--Soibelman $\rho$-completion)
for class~M unless raw finite propagation is separately verified.
$\Phi_{\mathrm{hol}}$ is functorial in maps of chiral algebras,
compatible with Drinfeld--Sokolov reduction, and applies on the
standard landscape with its stated analytic hypotheses: rational or
lisse $C_2$-cofinite loci, Virasoro and $\cW_3$ at generic
non-critical tempered central charge, principal $W_N$ on the
finite-envelope loci, Schellekens $c=24$ holomorphic, Monster
$V^\natural$, and irrational cosets $\mathrm{Com}(B,C)$.
Logarithmic $W(p)$ triplet tempering is open
(Conjecture~\ref{conj:tempered-stratum-contains-wp}:
Gurarie 1993 and Flohr 1996 exhibit logarithmic amplitudes
with unbounded Massey products despite finite-dimensional Zhu,
so the Zhu-bounded-Massey route does not close $W(p)$).
\emph{3d quantum gravity is the first non-trivial instance}
($\cA = \mathrm{Vir}_c$, $c \neq 0$ non-critical;
Corollary~\ref{cor:rung-virasoro}).
\emph{$E_\infty$-topological is the operadic horizon} via
iterated Sugawara on $W_\infty[\lambda]$
(Corollary~\ref{cor:rung-w-infty},
Theorem~\ref{thm:climax-w-infty-endpoint}).

The atom of the volume is the directed two-coloured dioperad $\SCchtop$
(closed colour the Fulton--MacPherson compactification $\FM_k(\C)$
of ordered configurations of $k$ points in the curve direction;
open colour the little-$1$-disks $\Eone(m)$ of $m$ ordered
intervals on the transverse $\R$; directional restriction
$\SCchtop(\dots,\mathsf{top},\dots;\mathsf{cl}) = \varnothing$
closing open inputs into closed outputs; an intermediary between
$E_1$-chiral and $E_3$-topological for which Dunn additivity does
\emph{not} apply). $\SCchtop$ is homotopy-Koszul with
$(\SCchtop)^! = (\mathsf{Lie},\mathsf{Ass},\text{shuffle-mixed})$
and admits seven equivalent presentations
(\emph{the heptagon},
Chapter~\ref{ch:sc-chtop-heptagon}): operadic, Koszul-dual,
factorisation, BV/BRST, $L_\infty$-convolution, Drinfeld-centre,
and derived-AG. The coloured coherence of $\SCchtop$ is the
$2$-cell structure of the underlying $(\infty,2)$-adjunction
$\barBch \dashv \Omegach$: bar and cobar are $1$-cells in an
$(\infty,2)$-category whose $2$-morphisms are the Swiss-cheese
colouring data, and the compatibility $\infty$-groupoid assembles as
$\mathrm{Data}(\cA) \simeq B\mathrm{Aut}^h_{L_\infty}(\fg^{\infty,1}_\cA)$
(with $\fg^{\infty,1}_\cA$ the $(\infty,1)$-convolution $L_\infty$-algebra
on $\Hom_\Sigma(B(\SCchtop), \End_\cA)$)
with Auslander--Reiten triangular structure on the homotopy
$2$-automorphisms. The cobar functor $\Omegach$ is left adjoint
to $\barBch$: on an $E_1$ dg coassociative coalgebra $\mathcal C$
it produces $\Omegach(\mathcal C) = T(s\bar{\mathcal C})$ with
differential summing the deconcatenation-dual Arnold
contractions. The reduced bar complex
\[
\barBch(\cA)=\bigoplus_{n\ge1}(s^{-1}\bar\cA)^{\otimes n}
\subset
\mathrm B^{\mathrm{ch}}(\cA)
=k\oplus\barBch(\cA)
\]
is the $E_1$ dg coassociative coalgebra over $(\mathsf{Ass}^{\mathrm{ch}})^!$
(differential $d_\cA$ from OPE residues on $\FM_k(\C)$,
deconcatenation coproduct $\Delta$ from $\Conf_k^<(\R)$;
the bar itself is not an $\SCchtop$-coalgebra). The bar
differential is the holomorphic factorisation map on
$\FM_k(\C)$, and \emph{chiral Kontsevich formality} holds on the
Koszul locus $U^{\mathrm{adm}}$:
$T^{\mathrm{poly}}_{\mathrm{ch}}|_{U^{\mathrm{adm}}}
\xrightarrow{\sim} \mathrm{ChirHoch}^\bullet|_{U^{\mathrm{adm}}}$
as $L_\infty$-quasi-isomorphism, the $n$-th obstruction on $H_n$
identified with the Malcev-ladder cocycle, promoted to an
$(\infty,1)$-lift via Francis--Gaitsgory factorisation
$\infty$-categories and assembled into an operadic formality in the
sense of Tamarkin. The $\SCchtop$ datum is borne by the chiral derived-centre pair
$(\cZ^{\mathrm{der}}_{\mathrm{ch}}(\cA),\,\cA)$
(the derived centre $\cZ^{\mathrm{der}}_{\mathrm{ch}}(\cA) =
\mathrm{RHom}_{\cA\otimes\cA^{\mathrm{op}}}(\cA,\cA)$, chiral Hochschild cochains):
$C^\bullet_{\mathrm{ch}}(\cA,\cA)$ acts on $\cA$ as the chiral
brace operad, the chain-level realisation of the Deligne conjecture. Three distinct
functors act on $\barBch(\cA)$:
$\Omega(\barBch(\cA)) \simeq \cA$ (inversion),
$\mathbb{D}_{\mathrm{Ran}}(\barBch(\cA)) \simeq \barBch(\cA^!)$
(Verdier),
$C^\bullet_{\mathrm{ch}}(\cA,\cA) \simeq
\cZ^{\mathrm{der}}_{\mathrm{ch}}(\cA)$ (derived centre).
The ordered bar is primitive; the symmetric bar
$B^\Sigma(\cA)$ of Volume~I is its
$\Sigma_n$-coinvariant shadow,
$\operatorname{av}\colon \mathfrak{g}^{E_1}_\cA \to
\mathfrak{g}^{\mathrm{mod}}_\cA$, and
$\kappa_{\mathrm{ch}}(\cA)$ is the leading scalar surviving
averaging. PVA descent extracts a $(-1)$-shifted Poisson vertex
algebra from the Stokes identity on $\FM_3(\C)$. Line operators
carry modules for $\cA^!_{\mathrm{line}}$; spectral braiding
satisfies Yang--Baxter; for affine Kac--Moody on evaluation
modules the braided line category is $\operatorname{Rep}_q(\fg)$
at $q = e^{i\pi/(k+h^\vee)}$
(Theorem~\ref{thm:affine-monodromy-identification}).
A \emph{universal ratio-of-levels law} holds on the
Fricke-reflective signature-$(2,n)$ rows
$\{\mathrm{II}_{2,2}, \mathrm{II}_{10,2}, \mathrm{II}_{18,2},
\mathrm{II}_{26,2}\}$ and the $24A_1$ Niemeier:
for Koszul chiral algebras $\cA_X, \cA_Y$ produced from
Calabi--Yau targets $X, Y$ by the Vol~III functor $\Phi$
with Fricke involution on $L_X, L_Y$,
the Lusztig quantisation levels $\ell_X, \ell_Y$ and
Mukai-lattice positive signatures $c_+(L_X), c_+(L_Y)$ satisfy
\[
 \ell_X/\ell_Y \;=\; c_+(L_X)/c_+(L_Y).
\]
(Positive-definite rows; Leech and Conway $V^{s\natural}$
on $\Lambda_{24}$; admit no Fricke involution; the ratio
is undefined there.)
The collision residue $r(z) = \Res^{\mathrm{coll}}_{0,2}(\Theta_\cA)$
is a $\mathrm{GRT}_1(\Q)$-torsor: nine canonical faces (the
classical seven plus Brown-motivic and Willwacher-operadic) read
through one bar hub.

Three inputs force Universal Holography.
\emph{Convergence}: shadow coefficients $S_r(\cA)$
(the shadow tower of moments of the Virasoro-type OPE kernel,
$\cA = \mathrm{Vir}_c$ giving
$(S_2, S_3, S_4, S_5) = (c/2, 2, 10/[c(5c+22)],
-48/[c^2(5c+22)])$)
satisfy $\limsup_{r\to\infty}(|S_r(\cA)|/r!)^{1/r} = 0$ with
Banach radius $\rho_*(\cA) = |c|/\beta_\cA$, where the Arnold
coupling $\beta_\cA$ is the leading $\FM_k(\C)$-pole order on the
Virasoro-type OPE kernel $\Theta_\cA$, on the proved or
finite-envelope loci:
$\beta_{\mathrm{Vir}} = \beta_{W_2} = 6$ and $\beta_{W_3}=10$ in the
proved low-rank cases; for principal $W_N$ the harmonic candidate
$\beta_{W_N} = 12(H_N-1)$, $H_N = \sum_{j=1}^N 1/j$
(so $\beta_{W_4}=13$) is conditional on the beta-law surface,
$\beta_{W(p)} = 4p-3$,
$\beta_{\mathrm{Com}} = 6\,|c_K(k)|$
(Theorems~\ref{thm:tempered-stratum-contains-virasoro},
\ref{thm:wn-tempered-all-N},
\ref{thm:irrational-coset-tempered};
Conjecture~\ref{conj:tempered-stratum-contains-wp}).
\emph{Rigidity}: bounded Zhu algebra
$A(\cA) = \cA / \cA[L_{-2} + L_{-1}]\cA$
(the associative finite-dimensional quotient encoding
admissible modules) forces bounded Massey products
$[m_k]_{k\ge 2}$ on the shadow-tower $A_\infty$-structure,
hence polynomial growth dominated by Stirling; the refined
VSKR + BGG criterion
(Verma-shadow-Kazhdan-ramification plus Bernstein--Gelfand--Gelfand
parabolic resolution, Corollary~\ref{cor:tempered-criterion-refined})
is strictly weaker than $C_2$-cofiniteness.
\emph{Gauge-theoretic}: Costello--Gwilliam factorisation
together with finite-orbifold BV descent.  For the Leech
$\mathbb Z/2$ orbifold the finite-group obstruction is the
Dijkgraaf--Witten class
$[\alpha_{\mathrm{orb}}]\in H^3(B\mathbb Z/2;\mathrm U(1))$;
finite-orbifold descent is the homotopy fixed point totalisation
$V^{hG}=\Tot[V\rightrightarrows\Map(G,V)\to\Map(G^2,V)\to\cdots]$,
with associator corrected by a local $2$-cochain $\beta$ when
$\alpha_{\mathrm{orb}}=d\beta$;
Monster descent uses the separate input
$[\alpha_{\mathrm{orb}}]=0$
(Proposition~\ref{prop:monster-alpha-explicit-zero}) and the FLM
boundary identification.  For the Schellekens $c=24$ rows, cyclic
orbifold classifications supply VOA presentations; $E_3$ descent is
invoked only after the three-stratum anomaly and BV-descent inputs in
the partition
$71 = 24_{\mathrm{Niemeier}} + 1_{V_1=0} + 46_{\mathrm{nonlat}}$
(Theorem~\ref{thm:schellekens-71-all-alpha-zero}).
The class~$\mathbf{M}$ global triangle
$\Abulk \simeq \cZ^{\mathrm{der}}_{\mathrm{ch}}(\Bbound) \simeq
\cZ^{\mathrm{der}}_{\mathrm{ch}}(\cC_{\mathrm{line}}) \simeq
\HH^\bullet_{\mathrm{ch}}(\cA^!_{\mathrm{line}})$
(with $\Abulk$ the 3d HT~bulk algebra, $\Bbound$ its boundary
$W$-algebra, $\cC_{\mathrm{line}}$ the category of line operators,
$\cA^!_{\mathrm{line}}$ the Koszul dual of the line-module algebra,
$\HH^\bullet_{\mathrm{ch}}$ the chiral Hochschild cohomology)
has a conditional cohomological core via the chiral Hochschild--DS
identification
$\mathrm{ChirHoch}^\bullet(W_k(\fg)) \simeq
H^\bullet_{\mathrm{DS}}(\mathrm{ChirHoch}^\bullet(V_k(\fg)))$
(Theorem~\ref{thm:chd-ds-hochschild},
Corollary~\ref{cor:universal-holography-class-M}) once the completed
bar-coalgebra SDR, coderivation transfer, and weight-completed HPL
convergence hypotheses are supplied: Drinfeld--Sokolov reduction then
commutes with chiral Hochschild cohomology on the stated principal and
hook-type \(W\)-algebra locus. Its chain-level mixed chiral-topological form is
the conditional statement in the weight-completed class~$\mathbf M$
ambient; the strict $E_3^{\mathrm{top}}$ lift is a structure on the
derived centre, not on the boundary algebra alone.

Each higher-spin Casimir
$T^{(n)} = [Q_{\mathrm{tot}}^{(n)},\,G^{(n)}]$
(with $G^{(n)}$ the $n$-th odd-spin current of the iterated
Sugawara ladder and $Q_{\mathrm{tot}}^{(n)}$ the cumulative
BRST differential through depth $n$) adds one $E_1$ factor by
Dunn additivity; at depth $N$ the derived centre is
$E_{N+1}$-topological
(Theorem~\ref{thm:iterated-sugawara-construction}). At the
endpoint $W_\infty[\lambda]$, $\lambda$ generic in the Prochazka
parameter locus $\Lambda_{\mathrm{gen}}$,
\[
 \cZ^{\mathrm{der}}_{\mathrm{ch}}(W_\infty[\lambda])
 \;\simeq\;
 \varprojlim_n \cZ^{\mathrm{der}}_{\mathrm{ch}}(W_n[\lambda])
\]
is $E_\infty$-topological
(Theorem~\ref{thm:climax-w-infty-endpoint}): three disjoint
convergence machineries (Prochazka triangular truncation,
Creutzig--Kanade--Linshaw parafermion, Pope--Romans--Shen with the
Bakas wedge algebra) meet at the uniform threshold
$N_0 = 2 w_{\max} - 1$, with Yamada uniqueness fixing the limit at
generic $\lambda$ ($\hypProchazka, \hypCKL, \hypPRSh, \hypYamada$).
The Virasoro $\lambda$-bracket
$\{T_\lambda T\} = \partial T + 2T\lambda + (c/12)\lambda^3$
generates the gravitational content: critical string at $c = 26$,
Koszul self-duality at $c = 13$, quartic contact
$\mathfrak{Q} = 10/[c(5c+22)]$ with Lee--Yang pole at
$c = -22/5$, the $\Ainf$ chiral ladder $(m_k)_{k\ge 2}$ as
graviton perturbation series, the soft-graviton identification
$m_r = \mathrm{sub}^{r-2}$-leading
(Theorem~\ref{thm:gravity-infinite-soft-tower}), perturbative
finiteness, and the Virasoro scalar bar trace
\(\Tr_{\mathrm{Bord}}(\Vir_c)\exp(\Theta)\).  This trace is the
perturbative thermal-AdS$_3$/BTZ seed after the Brown--Henneaux
dictionary and saddle labelling are supplied; it is not the
Maloney--Witten modular orbit sum.  The scalar genus series has
radius \(4\pi^2\) in \(\hbar^2\), and no Page or BTZ Stokes jump
follows from the scalar tower alone.  The Page time
$t_P = 3 S_{\mathrm{BH}}/13$
(Proposition~\ref{prop:gravity-page-curve}) belongs to the separate
raw-transseries model under sectorial Borel summability, Stokes,
modular-invariance, vacuum-dominance, and saddle-extraction
hypotheses; the conjectural de~Sitter
$S_{\mathrm{dS}} = \pi\ell/(2G)$ lane requires the analogous
Gibbons--Hawking saddle data.
3d quantum gravity is the $N = 2$ rung of the Sugawara ladder;
$W_\infty[\lambda]$ at generic Prochazka parameter is the
$E_\infty$-topological endpoint.

At genus~$1$ the bar differential is curved unconditionally,
$d^2_{\mathrm{fib}} = \kappa_{\mathrm{ch}}(\cA) \cdot \omega_1$
(with $\omega_1 = c_1(\lambda_1) \in H^2(\overline{\mathcal M}_{1,1})$
the first Chern class of the Hodge line bundle
$\lambda_1 = \pi_*\omega_\pi$ on the moduli of elliptic curves)
via twisted K\"unneth on $\FM_k(C_\tau)$
(Proposition~\ref{prop:genus1-twisted-tensor-product}). The
bar--cobar closure at codimension $k_{\max}(2) = 3$ on
$\overline{\mathcal A}_2 \setminus \bigcup_n H_n$ passes through
the \emph{octachotomy} of eight ambient $(\infty,1)$-categories
(fibrewise generic; single-monodromy $H_n$; bi-unipotent Malcev;
tri-unipotent Malcev at $(3,4,7)$; weight-completed coderived;
$\Ainf$-corrected; $(\infty,1)$-$\mathrm{Perf}(\overline{\mathcal A_2})$;
chiral-Kontsevich-formal on the Koszul locus $U^{\mathrm{adm}}$
--the locus where chirally Koszul structure is unobstructed)
each carrying the Maurer--Cartan closure $\mathrm{MC}(\overline
B^{(2)}(\cA))$ on distinct Humbert-divisor intersection data;
the universal closure depth at genus $g$ is
$k_{\max}(g) = g(g+1)/2$ (Chapter~\ref{ch:curved-dunn-higher-genus}).
For $g \ge 2$, curved-Dunn $H^2$ vanishing is conditional on
modular-bootstrap $H^2$ acyclicity together with the degree-$2$
comparison of the modular-bootstrap and curved-Dunn complexes
(Theorem~\ref{thm:curved-dunn-H2-vanishing-all-genera},
Chapter~\ref{ch:curved-dunn-higher-genus}). Irregular-singular
KZB theory supplies generic non-integral boundary composition only
after a compatible boundary formal type and mixed Stokes data are
given
(Theorem~\ref{thm:irregular-singular-kzb-regularity}); it does not
prove the curved-Dunn $H^2$ statement. The
modular operad $\SCchtop_{\mathrm{mod}}$ has composition at
integral level
(Theorem~\ref{thm:affine-composition-associativity}),
equivariance (Proposition~\ref{prop:qt-equivariance}), and
unitality (Proposition~\ref{prop:modular-operad-unitality})
proved at all genera. The conformal anomaly is the obstruction
to a constant coproduct; topologisation is proved only in the
ambient named by the theorem. Affine Kac--Moody at non-critical
level is controlled by Theorem~\ref{thm:E3-topological-km};
principal and non-principal Drinfeld--Sokolov $W$-algebras use the
total DS BRST ambient of
Theorems~\ref{thm:E3-topological-DS}
and~\ref{thm:E3-topological-DS-general}
through the \(\hypDSBRST\)-type cohomological identity
\(T_{\mathrm{DS}} = [Q_{\mathrm{DS}}, G'_{\mathrm{DS}}]\). Finite-type
freely generated finite-jet PVAs have a constructed classical
Khan--Zeng BV action, but their all-loop boundary vertex algebra and
$E_3$ lift remain conditional on the KZ analytic SDR package,
image-choice Stokes, reflected weights, and the lift of
$T=[Q_{\mathrm{tot}},G]$
(Theorem~\ref{thm:E3-topological-free-PVA}). Heisenberg and
lattice VOAs reach $E_3$-topological abelianly. The pair
remains at $\SCchtop$ (the generic case) at critical level
$k = -h^\vee$, where Sugawara degenerates and the centre
jumps to the Feigin--Frenkel oper centre.

The K3$\times E$ bridge has one proved scalar shadow and one
chain-level residual. The proved scalar shadow is the automorphic
identity
\begin{equation}
 Z^{K3\times E}_{\mathrm{BPS}}(Z)
 \;=\;
 \frac{1}{\Phi_{10}^{\mathrm{un}}(Z)}
 \;=\;
 \Delta_5(Z)^{-2},
 \qquad Z\in\mathfrak H_2,
\label{eq:abstract-climax}
\end{equation}
where $\Delta_5$ is the Gritsenko--Nikulin weight-$5$ square root
of the unnormalised Igusa cusp form
$\Phi_{10}^{\mathrm{un}}=\Delta_5^2$; equivalently
$\mathrm{wt}(\Delta_5)=c_{\phi_{0,1}^{K3}}(0)/2=10/2=5$, while
$\Phi_{10}^{\mathrm{un}}$ has weight~$10$. The G\"ottsche series is a one-variable
Euler-characteristic specialisation of the second-quantised K3
elliptic genus,
\[
 \sum_{N\ge 0}p^N\chi(\mathrm{Hilb}^N K3)
 \;=\;
 \prod_{m\ge 1}(1-p^m)^{-24};
\]
it is not the full genus-$2$ function~\eqref{eq:abstract-climax}.
The chain-level residual is the gravity-line Hall--Borcherds
comparison: not an equivalence
\[
\operatorname{Schur}\!\left(T[A_1,\Sigma_{0,24}]\right)
\cong \mathbf H_{\Delta_5},
\]
but a finite-height Hall/chiral gate.  The oriented $K3\times E$
Hall comparison must pass through the reduced compact Hall source,
finite radical-quotient Hall--Drinfeld doubles, height-compatible
Hall--Borcherds recognition, and the $\SCchtop$ bulk--boundary trace.
Equivalently,
\[
\operatorname{rad}_{\mathrm{Hall},N}/\operatorname{rad}_{N}=0,\quad
D^{\mathrm{fin}}_{\mathrm{Hall}}\ \text{exists},\quad
\operatorname{Borch}\circ\operatorname{Hall}\ \text{is height-compatible},
\quad
\operatorname{Schur}\to\Zderch\ \text{is }\SCchtop\text{-trace compatible}
\]
for every finite height~$N$. Its required operator form, still
conditional on these finite Hall and chiral-comparison gates, is the
Dirac--Igusa protected-Pfaffian datum
\[
 \mathfrak D_{K3\times E}\in
 \operatorname{End}_{\mathrm{ChirHoch}}\!\left(H_{K3\times E}\right)
 \quad\text{with}\quad
 \iota_{\mathrm{aut}}\!\left(\protectedPfaff{\mathfrak D_{K3\times E}}\right)
 =\Delta_5
\]
and the Hall/chiral square
\(I_{\mathrm{Hall}}\colon
\CoHA(K3)\to D_{\mathrm{Hall}}(K3)\to\mathfrak g_{\Delta_5}\)
against
\[
D^{b}\mathrm{Coh}(K3)
\xrightarrow{\ (-)\boxtimes\mathcal O_E\ }
D^{b}\mathrm{Coh}(K3\times E)
\xrightarrow{\ \PhiFA_{3}\ }
E_3\text{-}\mathrm{FactAlg}(K3\times E)
\xrightarrow{\ \SpCh_{E,C}\ }
\mathrm{ChirAlg}_C
\xrightarrow{\ \Zderch\ }
\Zderch(A_X)\subset\SCchtop\text{-}\mathrm{Alg},
\]
where \(A_X=\SpCh_{E,C}\PhiFA_3(D^b\mathrm{Coh}(K3\times E))\).
Conditional on that comparison, $\mathbf H_{\Delta_5}$ is the
Hall--Borcherds boundary object whose scalar Fock character realises the
shadow~\eqref{eq:abstract-climax}; the full $3$d gravitational
path integral lies beyond this scalar identity.

\textbf{Three-factor universal trace identity}
(Conjecture~\ref{conj:universal-trace-identity-volii-bridge},
\S\ref{sec:universal-trace-identity}). On the Koszul-self-dual
subcategory of objects admitting a BRST resolution with
Calabi--Yau target supporting a Borcherds product,
\[
 \underbrace{\mathrm{tr}_{\mathrm{ghost}}(Q_{\mathrm{BRST}}^2)}_{\text{Vol~I ghost-scope}}
 \;=\;\underbrace{\mathrm{tr}_{\mathrm{Pentagon}}}_{\text{Vol~II MC5 sewing of single-colour closed $\SCchtop$}}
 \;=\;\underbrace{\kappa_{\mathrm{BKM}}(\Phi_N)}_{\text{Vol~III Borcherds weight}}
 \;=\;c_N(0)/2,
\]
the Fourier zero-coefficient of the Borcherds lift $\Phi_N$
attached to the $N$th K3-fibered Class~A row, $N \in \{1, 2, 3, 4, 6\}$,
carrying $c_N(0)/2 \in (5, 2, 1, 1, 1/2)$
(\cref{conj:universal-trace-identity-volii-bridge}; the
CHL-averaged additive lift of Gritsenko~1999 Thm~1.2 supplies
the alternative family $\mathrm{wt}(\Delta_1^{(N)}) = (5, 4, 3, 2, 1)$,
which enters the Cl\'ery--Gritsenko 8-form atlas separately).
Convergence at $N=1$ gives the $\{5,5,5\}$ coincidence on the
K3 Heisenberg witness (ghost, Pentagon, Borcherds all equal~$5$).
The Mukai/conductor invariant $K^{\kappa_{\mathrm{ch}}}=8$ is a
separate Heisenberg--Mukai datum, not the Borcherds weight.
Scope: K3-fibered Class~A (the eight diagonal orbifolds plus
the STU model, for which Stage-2 specialisation produces a
chiral shadow on a reference elliptic curve); Class~B
($\kappa_{\mathrm{BCOV}} = \chi(X)/24$ Bershadsky--Cecotti--Ooguri--Vafa
invariant and shadow depth, for Calabi--Yau $X$ outside
the K3-fibered locus) lies outside. The proposed additive split of
the BKM weight into a chiral scalar plus $\chi(\cO_{\mathrm{fiber}})$
is false at every $N$ and does not appear. The three volumes meet at one
object $\cZ^{\mathrm{der}}_{\mathrm{ch}}(\cA)$ read through
three lenses: Volume~I algebraic (bar-cobar, Fuchsian ODE on
four Laurent tiers, Kummer-irregular arithmetic),
Volume~II physical (the $\mathsf{SC}^{\mathrm{ch,top}}$ bulk--boundary datum and
$3$d HT-QFT holography), Volume~III categorical (the
two-stage CY-to-chiral functor
$\Phi_d = {\SpCh}_{\Sigma_{d-1},C}\circ\PhiFA_d$ with
$E_n$-level dimension dependence on the stage-2 shadow,
the six-routes pentagon stratified by generator rank
$\rho^{R_i}$, the super-Riccati shadow tower with $\max(m,n)$
complementarity, the $CY_4$ $p_1$-twisted double current
algebra). \emph{Theorem H extends to CY-$d$ at Stage-$1$ and contracts
under Stage-$2$ specialisation.}
On the reference curve $C$ the chiral Hochschild cohomology
of a Koszul $\cA$ concentrates in degrees $\{0, 1, 2\}$; under
the Vol~III functor $\Phi_d = {\SpCh}_{\Sigma_{d-1},C}\circ\PhiFA_d$
the Stage-$1$ $E_d$-holomorphic factorisation algebra carries the
enlarged concentration $\{0, 1, 2, d\}$ on a CY-$d$ target, with
sharp vanishing $\mathrm{ChirHoch}^k = 0$ for $k \ge d + 1$.
Stage-$2$ specialisation ${\SpCh}_{\Sigma_{d-1},C}$ absorbs the
CY-fundamental class into the factorisation-homology pushforward
along $\Sigma_{d-1}$, recovering the Vol~II curve-side
concentration $\{0,1,2\}$. A \emph{CY-$4$ hyperk\"ahler-restricted conjecture}
(Conjecture~\ref{conj:universal-trace-identity-volii-bridge},
$\Phi_4$ on hyperk\"ahler $4$-folds $\mathrm{K3} \times
\mathrm{K3}$ and $\mathrm{Hilb}^2(\mathrm{K3})$) realises the
$3$d HT-QFT climax via twistor-$S^1$ reduction to
$3$d~$\cN=4$ gauge theory, lifted to the Costello--Gaiotto
$11$d holography, with paramodular pairing
$4\,\mathrm{Vol}(\mathrm{K3}_1)\,\mathrm{Vol}(\mathrm{K3}_2)\,(2\pi i)^4$
reached through DT-$4$ sheaf counting, Nekrasov $8$d
instanton partition function, and the DMVV product formula.

\textbf{Open frontier.}
Logarithmic $W(p)$ triplet tempering hinges on the
Adamovi\v{c}--Milas character-amplitude bound on the
boundary-changing field $\phi_{0,1}$, open on
$H^\bullet_{\mathrm{reg}}$.
The super-complementarity pairing
$\kappa_{\mathrm{ch}} + \kappa_{\mathrm{ch}}^! = \max(m,n)$
for super-Yangians on the sub-Sugawara line awaits a canonical
bridge between supertrace and Berezinian pairings.
At $g \ge 3$ the $\lambda_g$-residue on $\overline{\cM}_g$
(with $\lambda_g = c_g(\pi_*\omega_\pi)$ the top Hodge class
of the universal curve) governs the all-genera
clutching-uniqueness input to Theorem~D.
Periodic-CDG admissibility for all simply-laced types
(Vol~I Theorem~\ref{V1-thm:admissible-kl-bar-cobar-adjunction}),
chain-level class~$\mathbf{M}$ on the three equivalent pro /
$J$-adic / weight-completed ambients
(Vol~I Theorem~\ref{V1-thm:mc5-class-m-chain-level-pro-ambient}),
and the boundary-formal-type KZB composition criterion at
$\overline{\cM}_{g,n}$
(Theorem~\ref{thm:irregular-singular-kzb-regularity}) are theorem
statements at their stated hypotheses.

\smallskip

\noindent
The terminal scalar identity
$Z^{K3\times E}_{\mathrm{BPS}}=(\Phi_{10}^{\mathrm{un}})^{-1}$ is
the proved scalar component; its lift to a Vol~II gravity-line
partition function reduces to the Hall--Borcherds comparison
residual, and Vol~III lifts
the single reference curve $C$ to the two-stage factorisation
$\Phi_d = {\SpCh}_{\Sigma_{d-1},C} \circ \PhiFA_d$, so one
Calabi--Yau target $X$ carries a family of chiral shadows
parametrised by
$(\Sigma_{d-1},C) \in \mathrm{CycCurve}(X)$--the $(\infty,1)$-groupoid
of pairs of a smooth $(d-1)$-cycle $\Sigma_{d-1} \hookrightarrow X$
and a reference curve $C \hookrightarrow \Sigma_{d-1}$--and the
separate $\mathsf B$-row Mukai/conductor ceiling
$K^{\kappa_{\mathrm{ch}}} = 8$ recurs in the question of
which Borcherds weights $\kappa_{\mathrm{BKM}}(\Phi_N)$ realise
Mukai-enhanced ghost traces on each row of the $\Psi$-sibling
landscape $\{\Psi,\Psi^{\deg},\Psi^{\mathrm{tor}},\Psi^{\mathrm{metap}}\}$
of GN-reflective signature-$(2,n\!\ge\!3)$ BKMs.
\end{abstract}

\section*{Notation and conventions}
\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=2em]
\item All algebraic structures are over $\C$ unless stated otherwise.
\item Grading is \emph{cohomological}: differentials have degree $+1$.
\item $\Ainf$ denotes the $A_\infty$ operad; $\Eone$, $\Etwo$, $\Einf$ the little-disks operads.
\item $\SCchtop$ is the holomorphic--topological Swiss-cheese \emph{coloured dioperad} in the sense of \cite{Gan03} (Definition~\ref{def:SC}; cf.\ Remark~\ref{rem:sc-chtop-not-operad} for why the object is not an operad in the standard sense).
\item $\FM(X)$ denotes the Fulton--MacPherson compactification of $\Conf(X)$.
\item $m_k$ are the $\Ainf$ chiral operations; $\lambda_i$ are spectral parameters.
\item Volume~I notation: $\cA$ for chiral algebras, $\barB(\cA)$ for bar, $\Omegach(C)$ for cobar, $\cA^!$ for Koszul dual; on the two-color Vol~II surface we write $\cA^!_{\mathrm{ch}}$ and $\cA^!_{\mathrm{line}}$ for the closed- and open-color duals.
\item \emph{Bicoloured primitive package}: $\primPkgBicolour$
on $(C, \R)$ with components $\openFactCat$ (open factorisation dg-cat on $\logCurve$),
$\cD^{\mathrm{cl}}|_X$ (closed factorisation $\infty$-cat), $\bdyVac$ (boundary vacuum),
$\bdyAlg = A_b = \mathrm{End}_{\openFactCat}(b)$ (chart algebra), $F^{\mathrm{cl}}$ (closed-colour vacuum FA),
$\HalfBraid$ (half-braiding), $\mathrm{tr}^{\mathrm{cl}}_X$ (closed-colour trace), $\TraceC$ (open-colour cyclic trace);
the $\kappa$-tuple
$\kappaTuple{\cA} = (\kappaCat, \kappaChHodge, \kappaChHeis, \kappaBKM, \kappaFiber)$
(Hodge / Heisenberg / BKM / Fiber rows; bare $\kappa$ forbidden);
the bar coalgebra $\BarTwc{\cA}$ (twisting/coupling shadow at level $\mathsf{S}$, \emph{not} the bulk);
the bulk $\bulkOf{\cA} = \bulkChirHoch{\cA} = \mathrm{ChirHoch}^\bullet(\cA, \cA)$ at level $\mathsf{Z}$;
the two-stage CY-chiral functor $\Phi_d = \SpCh \circ \PhiFA_d$;
the conditional-locus tags $\hyp\{\mathrm{Prochazka, CKL, PRSh, Yamada, KZSDR, Stokes, ReflWts, TLift, AmbientWtCpl, HTGlobalDR, BHdict, ModInv, VacDom, SadDom\}}$;
the effectiveness tags $\eff\{\mathrm{Koszul, PfaffOrient, HCSQuartic, PBWnoExtra, MOcocycle\}}$;
the universal stage chain $\mathsf{P} \to \mathsf{C} \to \mathsf{S} \to \mathsf{Z} \to \mathsf{A}$ with licensing tags $\alpha / \beta / \gamma / \delta / \epsilon$ inline at every theorem statement.
\end{itemize}

\subsection*{What this volume assumes from Volume~I}

Volume~I proves five structural theorems for chiral algebras on
curves. This volume depends on all five.

\begin{enumerate}[label=\textbf{(\Alph*)},leftmargin=2.2em]
\item \textbf{Bar--cobar adjunction.}
 The geometric bar complex $\barB_X(\cA)$ and cobar complex
 $\Omegach(\barB_X(\cA))$ form an adjoint pair of functors on
 chiral algebras. Volume~II reinterprets the bar complex as an
 algebra over the Swiss-cheese operad
 $\mathsf{SC}^{\mathrm{ch,top}}$; the adjunction is assumed, not
 reproved.
\item \textbf{Bar--cobar inversion.}
 On the Koszul locus the counit
 $\Omegach(\barB_X(\cA))\to \cA$ is a quasi-isomorphism.
 Volume~II lifts this to the raviolo vertex algebra setting and to
 completed towers via weightwise stabilization.
\item \textbf{Complementarity.}
 The deformation and obstruction complexes $\mathbf Q_g(\cA)$ and
 $\mathbf Q_g(\cA^!)$ are complementary Lagrangians inside the
 shifted-symplectic ambient complex
 $R\Gamma(\overline{\mathcal M}_g,\mathcal Z(\cA))$. The
 $(-1)$-shifted symplectic structure is the genus-tower input for
 the curved Swiss-cheese algebras of Part~\ref{part:swiss-cheese}.
\item \textbf{Modular characteristic.}
 The scalar $\kappa(\cA)$ and the $\hat A$-genus control the
 curvature $\dfib^{\,2}=\kappa(\cA)\cdot\omega_g$ of the genus
 tower. The spectral discriminant $\Delta_\cA$ is the first
 non-scalar invariant.
\item \textbf{Hochschild duality.}
 The chiral Hochschild cochain complex
 $\mathrm{ChirHoch}^*(\cA)$ has amplitude $[0,2]$ with a Hilbert
 polynomial in $t$ on the Koszul locus at generic level and is dual
 to $\mathrm{ChirHoch}^*(\cA^!)$. The critical
 level $k = -h^\vee$ is excluded because $\dim \mathrm{ChirHoch}^0$ can
 be infinite there (see Vol~I Remark~\ref*{rem:critical-level-lie-vs-chirhoch}).
 It is the natural candidate for the bulk algebra in the $3$d
 holomorphic-topological theory.
\end{enumerate}
Volume~II computes the three-dimensional content of these five
theorems through the Swiss-cheese lens.

\subsection*{Results by scope}
\label{subsec:scope-stratification}

The theorem surface separates by scope. The operadic
foundations split: homotopy-Koszulity and the Koszul-dual operad
calculus are algebraic, while product-cover descent and recognition
identify only the product-formal local shadow of HT factorization
data. On the chirally Koszul locus, the
line-module comparison, Yangian recognition, and duality
involution are proved algebraically. For physical
realisations supplied by Theorem~\ref{thm:physics-bridge}, the
standard affine HT gauge realization gives
Theorem~\ref{thm:Koszul_dual_Yangian}: the field-theoretic
open-colour Koszul dual carries the dg-shifted Yangian
package.
The standing hypotheses (H1)--(H4) are distributed across the
algebraic and physical inputs: the algebraic framework is
built on the definition of a logarithmic $\SCchtop$-algebra
(Definition~\ref{def:log-SC-algebra}), the geometric FM calculus
(Theorem~\ref{thm:FM-calculus}), the product-formal local-shadow
recognition statement (Theorem~\ref{thm:recognition-SC}), and the
physical bridge theorem (Theorem~\ref{thm:physics-bridge}). Physical
realisations must verify the product HT gauge data and the factorized
logarithmic parametrix.

\begin{remark}[Scope of results]
\label{rem:conditional-list}
The algebraic framework separates as follows:
\begin{itemize}
\item \emph{Unconditional for any logarithmic $\SCchtop$-algebra}
 \emph{(}Definition~\ref{def:log-SC-algebra}\emph{):}
 Proposition~\ref{prop:compatibility} (prefactorization $\Rightarrow$
 $W(\SCchtop)$-algebra),
 Theorem~\ref{thm:scchtop-bar-cobar-adjunction} (bar--cobar Quillen
 equivalence),
 Theorem~\ref{thm:filtered-koszul} (filtered Quillen equivalence),
 Proposition~\ref{prop:sc-koszul-duality-nonselfdual} (Koszul dual
 of~$\SCchtop$).
\item \emph{On the chirally Koszul locus:}
 Theorem~\ref{thm:lines_as_modules}
 ($\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{line}} \simeq
 \cA^!_{\mathrm{line}}\text{-}\mathbf{mod}$),
 Theorem~\ref{thm:yangian-recognition}
 (the open-colour Koszul dual carries the dg-shifted Yangian
 package),
	 Theorem~\ref{thm:duality-involution}
	 (homotopy Koszul duality is involutive, and the small involution
	 is strict SC-formal).
\item \emph{For the standard affine HT gauge realization among the
 physical examples supplied by
 Theorem~\ref{thm:physics-bridge}:}
 Theorem~\ref{thm:Obs-is-SC}
 (BV observables as Swiss-cheese algebra),
 Theorem~\ref{thm:bulk-CHC}
 (local bulk observables $\simeq$ chiral Hochschild cochains),
 Theorem~\ref{thm:Koszul_dual_Yangian}
 (the field-theoretic open-colour Koszul dual is a
 dg-shifted Yangian).
\item \emph{Core constructions on the algebraic surface:}
 The chain-level $\Ainf$ structure, PVA descent, spectral
 $R$-matrix, and the Swiss-cheese descent package are built for
 logarithmic $\SCchtop$-algebras; the standing
 hypotheses are distributed between the algebraic axioms, the
 local-shadow recognition theorem, and the physics bridge.
 The standing hypotheses (H1)--(H4) are distributed as follows:
 (H1) and (H2) are hypotheses on physical realisations
 (Theorem~\ref{thm:physics-bridge}); (H3) is the FM calculus
 theorem; (H4) is the product-formal local-shadow recognition
 statement.
 The worked examples verify that specific gauge theories
 satisfy Theorem~\ref{thm:physics-bridge}.
\end{itemize}
\end{remark}

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
\part{The Open Primitive}
\label{part:swiss-cheese}
\phantomsection\label{part:open-primitive} % alias for consumer refs using pre-healed name
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

\noindent
The primitive datum of a three-dimensional holomorphic--topological
theory on $\C_z \times \R_t$ is not an algebra but the bicoloured
nine-tuple
$\primPkgBicolour$
on the curve--line pair $(C, \R)$
\textup(\cref{def:bicoloured-primitive-package},
\cref{thm:bicoloured-primitive-universality}\textup): the open
factorisation dg-category $\openFactCat$ on the tangentially
decorated curve $\logCurve = (X, D, \tau)$, the closed
factorisation $\infty$-category $\cD^{\mathrm{cl}}|_X$, a chosen
boundary vacuum $\bdyVac = b$, the chart algebra
$\bdyAlg = A_b = \End_{\openFactCat}(b)$, the closed-colour vacuum
factorisation algebra $F^{\mathrm{cl}}$, the half-braiding
$\HalfBraid$ mediating bulk-to-boundary OPE residue, the
closed-colour trace $\mathrm{tr}^{\mathrm{cl}}_X$ evaluating to
$c_N(0)/2$ at the Pentagon endpoint, and the open-colour cyclic
trace $\TraceC$. The Morita class of $\openFactCat$, not the
quasi-isomorphism class of $A_b$, is the invariant; $A_b$ is the
level-$\mathsf{C}$ chart projection of the level-$\mathsf{P}$
primitive. The governing operadic structure is the two-coloured
Swiss-cheese operad $\SCchtop$ with closed colour $\FM_k(\C)$
\textup(holomorphic collisions\textup) and open colour $E_1(m)$
\textup(topological orderings\textup), with directional
restriction
$\SCchtop(\ldots, \mathsf{top}, \ldots; \mathsf{cl}) = \varnothing$.
The bar coalgebra $\BarTwc{A_b}$ of Volume~I is the
level-$\mathsf{S}$ shadow of $A_b$, encoding twisting morphisms;
it is \emph{not} the bulk
\textup(\cref{rem:bar-vs-centre-canonical}\textup). The bulk is
the level-$\mathsf{Z}$ derived chiral centre
$\bulkOf{A_b} \simeq \bulkChirHoch{A_b} =
\mathrm{ChirHoch}^\bullet(A_b, A_b)$, the universal algebra of
operators acting on the boundary from the interior
\textup(\S\ref{subsec:categorical-open-sector},
\cref{thm:cyclic-hochschild-stage-stratification}\textup).

The operadic machine then immediately yields its classical shadow.
The regular part of $m_2$ is a commutative product, the singular
part is a $\lambda$-bracket, and together they form a
$(-1)$-shifted Poisson vertex algebra on $H^\bullet(A_b, Q)$,
proved by the same Arnold relations and Stokes identities that
govern $d_{\BarTwc{}}^2 = 0$ in Volume~I. The raviolo vertex
algebra is the algebraic avatar of the $\C \times \R$ geometry,
constructed from the Swiss-cheese structure via
\v{C}ech/Thom--Sullivan descent.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
\part{The $E_1$ Core}
\label{part:e1-core}
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

\noindent
Part~\ref{part:open-primitive} constructed the bicoloured primitive
package $\primPkgBicolour$ on $(C, \R)$ and the $\SCchtop$ dioperad
with its heptagon of presentations
\textup(\cref{def:bicoloured-primitive-package},
\cref{thm:bicoloured-primitive-universality}\textup). Choosing a
boundary vacuum $b \in \openFactCat$ projects the level-$\mathsf{P}$
primitive to the level-$\mathsf{C}$ chart algebra
$\bdyAlg = A_b = \mathrm{End}_{\openFactCat}(b)$. The closed-colour
side of the chart carries the symmetric bar $B^\Sigma(A_b)$ of
Vol~I Theorem~A. The open-colour side carries the \emph{ordered}
bar $B^{\mathrm{ord}}(A_b) = T^c(s^{-1}\overline{A_b})$ with
deconcatenation coproduct. Descent
$B^\Sigma_n(\cA) \simeq (\pi_{n,*}\,B^{\mathrm{ord}}_n(\cA))^{\Sigma_n,\,\cL_R}$
is $R$-twisted (Proposition~\ref{prop:R-twisted-sigma-n-descent}):
the $R$-matrix $R(z) \in \End(B^{\mathrm{ord}}_2)$, the KZ
associator, and the full Yangian deformation survive on the
ordered side; the $\Sigma_n$-coinvariant projection
$\mathrm{av}\colon B^{\mathrm{ord}} \to B^\Sigma$ annihilates them
at the pole-free reduction $R(z) = \tau$ (classical
Loday--Vallette). The Hodge-row component $\kappaChHodge(\cA)$ of the
$\kappa$-tuple
\textup(equal to $\mathrm{av}(r(z))$ for abelian algebras;
$\mathrm{av}(r(z)) + \dim(\fg)/2$ for non-abelian Kac--Moody\textup)
is the leading scalar shadow of the collision residue
$r(z) = \Res^{\mathrm{coll}}_{0,2}(\Theta_\cA)$ whose nine
$\mathrm{GRT}_1(\Q)$-torsor faces are the subject of
Part~\ref{part:char-datum}; the full five-row $\kappaTuple{\cA}$
discipline lives there. The unified chiral quantum group
$Q_\fg^{k,f,\mu}$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:Koszul_dual_Yangian})
packages the $E_1$-core data of the ordered side: spectral
$R$-matrix quasi-triangularity, CYBE, co-associativity, Chevalley
$\hbar\mapsto-\hbar$ Koszul self-duality. The spectral Drinfeld
strictification is controlled by the root-sector scalar classes
$[\epsilon_{\alpha,n}]\in
\mathscr K_n/\operatorname{im}(\delta_{\alpha,n})$: root
multiplicity one plus Jacobi collapse
(Theorem~\ref{thm:adjacent-root-rigidity}) scalarises each finite
root-sector obstruction, while the $1/n$ BCH calculation kills the
path sectors. Strictification is therefore the scalar-vanishing
locus, not a consequence of multiplicity one alone. The annular gluing
$B^{\mathrm{ann}}(\cA)$ closes the two-sided bar complex into
$\ChirHoch^\bullet(\cA, \cA) = \bulkChirHoch{\cA}$: the
level-$\mathsf{Z}$ centre, identified with the bulk via Theorem~H
\textup(\cref{thm:annular-HH-vol2},
\cref{thm:cyclic-hochschild-stage-stratification}\textup); used
to construct the universal bulk in Part~\ref{part:char-datum}.

\part{The Topologisation Ladder}
\label{part:topologisation-ladder}
\phantomsection\label{part:bbl-core} % alias preserved for legacy references to former Part III
\phantomsection\label{part:seven-faces} % alias preserved (r(z) torsor moved to Part IV)
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

\noindent
The chart functor of Part~\ref{part:e1-core} carries an iterated
Sugawara dynamics. A conformal vector $\omega \in A_b$ at non-critical
level $k \ne -h^\vee$ exhibits the chart algebra
$A_b$ as $E_1$-chiral promoted to $E_3^{\mathrm{top}}$ on
$Q$-cohomology by the Sugawara identification
$T(z) = [Q_{\mathrm{tot}}, G(z)]$ with $G(z)$ the antighost partner.
Each additional inner higher-spin Casimir
$T^{(n)} = [Q_{\mathrm{tot}}, G^{(n)}]$ promotes $E_{k+1}^{\mathrm{top}}$
to $E_{k+2}^{\mathrm{top}}$ by Dunn additivity, building a
ladder
$E_1 \to E_3^{\mathrm{top}} \to E_4^{\mathrm{top}} \to \cdots
 \to E_{N+1}^{\mathrm{top}}$
at $W_N$ chart algebras, and the inverse-limit endpoint
$E_\infty^{\mathrm{top}}$ at $A_b = \cW_\infty[\lambda]$ conditional
on the four standing hypotheses
$\hypProchazka, \hypCKL, \hypPRSh, \hypYamada$.
Curved-Dunn $H^2 = 0$ at $g \ge 2$ secures chain-level
modular composition; the original-complex dichotomy on class
$\mathsf{M}$ separates the tempered locus from non-tempered
exceptions, and the strict Mittag--Leffler weight-completed
ambient $\hypAmbientWtCpl$ supplies the ambient where the
$\cW_N$ tempering closure $\beta_N = 12(H_N - 1)$ is finite.
The ladder is the dynamical content of $\SCchtop$: the same
operadic primitive of Part~\ref{part:swiss-cheese}, dynamically
promoted by an inner stress tower.

\part{The Characteristic Datum}
\label{part:char-datum}
\phantomsection\label{part:examples} % alias preserved for legacy refs (κ-tuple landscape lives here)
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

\noindent
The chart functor of Part~\ref{part:e1-core} and its dynamical
ladder of Part~\ref{part:topologisation-ladder} produce the
universal Maurer--Cartan element
$\Theta_\cA \in \mc(\gAmod)$ whose collision residue
$r(z) = \mathrm{Res}^{\mathrm{coll}}_{0,2}(\Theta_\cA)$ has nine
canonical $\mathrm{GRT}_1(\Q)$-orbit representatives
$F_1, \ldots, F_9$ \textup(F1 bar hub; F2 DNP $R$-twist;
F3 BBL slab $\Abulk \simeq \Zder(\Bbound)$; F4 celestial soft
factors $r(z) \sim S_0/z + S_1/z^2 + \cdots$; F5 affine
half-space BV $\barB^{\mathrm{ch}}(\widehat{\fg}_k)$; F6
planted-forest synthesis on $\FM_3(X)$; F7 Kontsevich integral;
F8 Brown motivic $r^{\mathrm{mot}} = r^{\mathrm{rat}} +
\sum_{w \ge 3 \text{ odd}} \zeta(w)\,r^{(w)}$ generating
$\operatorname{Lie}(\mathfrak{grt}_1)^\vee$; F9 Willwacher operadic
$H^0(\mathrm{GC}_2) = \mathfrak{grt}_1$\textup). Yang--Baxter
$R_{12} R_{13} R_{23} = R_{23} R_{13} R_{12}$ is Stokes on
$\FM_3(\C)$; the classical limit is the Sklyanin Jacobi identity.
The five-row $\kappa$-tuple
$\kappaTuple{\cA} =
(\kappaCat, \kappaChHodge, \kappaChHeis, \kappaBKM, \kappaFiber)(\cA)$
is the genuine modular characteristic
\textup(no scalar $\kappa$\textup); each row is a distinct
projection of the curvature class
$d_B^2 = \kappaChHodge \cdot \omega_g$ on the uniform-weight locus,
lifted to the tensor-Arakelov class
$K(\cA) \in \operatorname{Sym}^2(\cF^\vee) \otimes
\Omega^2(\overline{\cM}_{g,n})$. The five-archetype landscape
$\mathsf{G/L/C/M/B}$ \textup(plus the $\mathsf{FF}$ Feigin--Frenkel
critical companion\textup) is the κ-supported partition; the
derived-centre complementarity ceiling
$\rho_K = \kappaChHodge(A) + \kappaChHodge(A^!) \in
\{0, 8, 13, 250/3, 98/3\}$ is Theorem~C
\textup(\cref{thm:theoremC-total-shifted-symplectic}\textup);
the obstruction-tower scalar formula is Theorem~D. The Heisenberg
Rosetta Stone computes every slot by hand. Closing this part:
the K3$\times E$ counterexample
\textup(\cref{thm:kappa-tuple-primitivity-orthogonal-shimura}\textup),
where the additive identity
$\kappaBKM = \kappaChHodge + \chi(\cO_{\mathrm{fiber}})$ \emph{fails}
at every $N \in \{1, 2, 3, 4, 6\}$, establishes the κ-tuple as
genuinely primitive: no scalar collapse holds.

\part{The Standard HT Landscape}
\label{part:holography}
\phantomsection\label{part:ht-landscape} % alias for consumer refs using pre-healed name
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

\noindent
Part~\ref{part:char-datum} produced the universal Maurer--Cartan
element $\Theta_\cA$, its collision residue
$r(z) = \mathrm{Res}^{\mathrm{coll}}_{0,2}(\Theta_\cA)$ on
the $\mathrm{GRT}_1(\Q)$-torsor of nine canonical faces, and the
five-row $\kappa$-tuple
$\kappaTuple{\cA} = (\kappaCat, \kappaChHodge, \kappaChHeis, \kappaBKM, \kappaFiber)$
with the K3$\times E$ counterexample to additivity establishing
its primitivity. Part~V populates the standard HT landscape
family-by-family. The transgression
algebra $B_\Theta(\cA) := \operatorname{Tot}^\Pi
\bigoplus_{g \ge 1} F_g(\cA) \cdot \hbar^{2g-2}$ packages the
genus tower
$F_g(\cA) =
\kappaChHodge(\cA)\lambda_g^{\mathrm{FP}}
{}+\delta F_g^{\mathrm{cross}}(\cA)$, where
$\lambda_g^{\mathrm{FP}}
=\int_{\overline{\cM}_{g,1}}\psi_1^{2g-2}\lambda_g$
and $\delta F_g^{\mathrm{cross}}$ vanishes on the uniform-weight
lane, into a single dg algebra with genus-Clifford completion at
$u = \eta^2$ (secondary anomaly class). Physical origins
(Chapter~\ref{chap:ht-physical-origins}) derive the $3$d HT twist
from either the $4$d $\cN = 2$ topological-holomorphic twist of
Costello--Gwilliam or the $(2,0)$ six-dimensional reduction. The
HT bulk--boundary line operators
(\cref{chap:ht-bulk-boundary-line-core}), the affine half-space
BV F5-face (\cref{chap:affine-half-space-bv}), and the celestial
boundary transfer F4-face (\cref{chap:celestial-boundary-transfer-core})
realise three of the nine $r(z)$-torsor faces directly on landscape
algebras. Yang--Mills boundary (class~$\mathbf{L}$, Sugawara
$T = -\tfrac{1}{2(k+h^\vee)} :J^a J_a:$) occupies disk-level
direct data. Celestial holography realises the soft-factor
shadow expansion $r(z) = S_0/z + S_1/z^2 + \cdots$. Logarithmic
HT monodromy extends the shifted KZ connection to an
$\Ainf$-superconnection on ordered configuration spaces. The
anomaly-completed package closes the triangle
$(\bdyAlg, \Zderch(\bdyAlg) = \bulkChirHoch{\bdyAlg}, \mathrm{Line}(\bdyAlg))$
into a single modular MC element $\Theta^{\mathrm{oc}}$, the
input to the Part~\ref{part:gravity} Virasoro specialisation.
6d holomorphic Chern--Simons supplies the gauge-theoretic quartic
anomaly class $\int_X \mathrm{Tr}_{\mathrm{ad}} A (F_A)^3$ and its
trace-factorisation/Green--Schwarz cancellation criterion; on
K3$\times E$, the Euler factor
$\int_{K3}c_2(TK3)=24$ supplies the cross-volume bridge to the
Hall--Borcherds construction of Vol~III. The shadow tower detects the
residual quartic class but does not itself construct the cancellation.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
\part{Three-Dimensional Quantum Gravity}
\label{part:gravity}
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

\noindent
The four preceding parts assemble the apparatus: Part~\ref{part:open-primitive}
the bicoloured primitive package; Part~\ref{part:e1-core} the
chart functor and bar coalgebra; Part~\ref{part:topologisation-ladder}
the iterated Sugawara dynamics promoting $E_1 \to E_n^{\mathrm{top}}$;
Part~\ref{part:char-datum} the $\kappa$-tuple, the nine
$r(z)$-torsor faces, and the $\mathsf{G/L/C/M/B}$ partition;
Part~\ref{part:ht-landscape} the standard HT landscape
family-by-family. Part~\ref{part:gravity} delivers the climax: at
the Brown--Henneaux specialisation
$\cA = \mathrm{Vir}_c$, $c = 3\ell/(2G_N)$,
\[
\boxed{\;
\Phihol(\mathrm{Vir}_c) \;=\; T_{\mathrm{Vir}_c},
\qquad
\Obs^{\mathrm{bulk}}(T_{\mathrm{Vir}_c})
 \;\simeq\;
 \Zderch(\mathrm{Vir}_c)
\;}
\]
is the $3$d holomorphic--topological gauge theory whose bulk
factorisation algebra is the chiral derived centre of Virasoro
(Universal Holography
\cref{thm:universal-holography-master}; cross-volume factorisation
$\Phihol = \Phi^{\mathrm{HT}}_{\mathrm{bic}} \circ
\mathsf{Prim}^{\mathrm{bic}}$ via
\cref{cor:universal-holography-via-bicoloured-universality}).
The fingerprint $\varphi'(\mathrm{Vir}_c) =
(4, \infty, +, 1, \mathrm{Vir}\text{-trivial}, c/2)$ places the
algebra in class~$\mathbf{M}$
\textup(\cref{thm:fingerprint-complete-ch}\textup): $p_{\max} = 4$
since the $\lambda$-bracket
$\{T_\lambda T\} = \partial T + 2 T \lambda + (c/12) \lambda^3$
has quartic pole; $r_{\max} = \infty$ so the shadow tower does
not truncate. Drinfeld--Sokolov reduction from
$\widehat{\fsl}_2$ at $k$ with $c = 1 - 6(k+1)^2/(k+2)$
\textup(\cref{thm:E3-topological-DS-general}\textup) reaches
the $E_3^{\mathrm{top}}$ rung; chain-level closure is
$\ChirHoch^\bullet(\mathrm{Vir}_c) \simeq
H^\bullet_{\mathrm{DS}}(\ChirHoch^\bullet(V_k(\fsl_2)))$
\textup(\cref{thm:chd-ds-hochschild}\textup). Chiral Higher Deligne
concentrates the derived centre in degrees $\{0, 1, 2\}$ with
$E_3$-chiral brace action
\textup(\cref{thm:chiral-higher-deligne}\textup); for $\mathrm{Vir}_c$
at generic $c$ the universal central-extension sector has
$\dim \ChirHoch^0_{\mathrm{cent}} = 1$,
$\dim \ChirHoch^1_{\mathrm{cent}} = 0$,
$\dim \ChirHoch^2_{\mathrm{cent}} = 1$
\textup(\cref{thm:chirhoch-virasoro-concentration}\textup), with
the Gel'fand--Fuchs class $\Theta_c$ representing the fixed-fibre
central-charge deformation. Virasoro occupies the $N = 2$ rung of the
$E_\infty$-topological ladder constructed in
Part~\ref{part:topologisation-ladder}; higher rungs
$\cW_N \to E_{N+1}^{\mathrm{top}}$ realise the Platonic climax's
extension into the Frontier. The four open Construction Problems
CP1--CP4 \textup(\cref{rem:four-construction-problems}\textup)
are the operator-level boundary objects of this part.

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
\part{The Frontier}
\label{part:frontier}
\phantomsection\label{part:v2-frontier} % alias for consumer refs using pre-healed name
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%

\noindent
Part~\ref{part:gravity} closed the $E_3^{\mathrm{top}}$-rung of
Virasoro and stated the $E_\infty^{\mathrm{top}}$-ladder
Platonic target. Part~VII contains the four irreducible boundary
faces of the present theorem, plus the four operator-level
Construction Problems CP1--CP4
\textup{(}\cref{rem:four-construction-problems},
\cref{thm:construction-problem-stage-stratification}\textup{)}.
The fronts are: \textup{(F1)} curved-Dunn $H^2 = 0$ at $g \ge 2$
under modular-bootstrap acyclicity + degree-$2$ comparison
\textup{(}\cref{thm:curved-dunn-H2-vanishing-all-genera}\textup{)};
\textup{(F2)} DS--Hochschild compatibility for class-$\mathbf{M}$
\textup{(}\cref{thm:chd-ds-hochschild}\textup{)}; \textup{(F3)}
periodic-CDG at admissible level
\textup{(}Vol~I~\cref{V1-thm:periodic-cdg-is-koszul-compatible}\textup{)};
\textup{(F4)} chain-level chiral Deligne--Tamarkin after
associator fix \textup{(}\cref{thm:chd-deligne-tamarkin}\textup{)}.
The Construction Problems are operator-level lifts of currently
scalar / shadow / classical / injection statements:
$\mathfrak D_X\in\operatorname{End}_{\mathrm{ChirHoch}}(H_X)$ on
$K3 \times E$ with
$\iota_{\mathrm{aut}}(\protectedPfaff{\mathfrak D_X})=\Deltafive$
\textup{(CP1)};
gravity-line operator algebra with Pentagon-face scalar trace
$\Phitenun = \Deltafive^2$ \textup{(CP2)}; unified PVA-quantum HT
theory \textup{(CP3)}; chiral Positselski extending Vol~I
Theorem~B at chiral generality \textup{(CP4,
\cref{thm:chiral-positselski-cross-volume}\textup{)}}. The part
collects four classes of chapters: \textup{(i)} conditional
statements depending on unproved analytic input outside the
logarithmic $\SCchtop$ framework
\textup{(}spectral-braiding frontier, celestial frontier, log HT
monodromy frontier, anomaly-completed frontier,
ordered-associative-KD frontier\textup{)}; \textup{(ii)}
conjectural chain-level extensions
\textup{(}modular PVA quantisation frontier, $\cW$-algebra
frontier\textup{)}; \textup{(iii)} cross-volume scope-boundary
items \textup{(}$W(p)$ tempering on $H^\bullet_{\mathrm{reg}}$,
super-complementarity canonical pairing, $\lambda_g$-conjecture
residue at $g \ge 3$\textup{)}; \textup{(iv)}
examples-complete-conditional. No earlier part depends on
material in this part. Closing section
\emph{Closures and re-openings}
\textup{(}absorbed from former Part~VIII per the platonic
seven-part spec\textup{)} records proved-and-conditional closure
statements for the four fronts.

% Closing section of Part VII (formerly Part VIII -- folded per the
% platonic seven-part form: closures and re-openings belong as the
% closing arc of the frontier, not a separate part).  Records proved
% and conditional closure statements for the four irreducible fronts
% (curved-Dunn $H^2$, class M, periodic-CDG admissible, chiral
% Deligne-Tamarkin) plus the residual three directions (F1', F4', F6)
% on which the programme meets its scope boundary.  Beyond these,
% the four open Construction Problems CP1-CP4 of CLAUDE.md sec 9 are
% the operator-level deliverables and live as the closing remark of
% Part VI (programme_climax_platonic.tex:rem:four-construction-problems).
\section*{Closures and re-openings}
\label{sec:closures-and-re-openings}
This closing section records proved and conditional closure
statements for the four genuine frontiers that seeded the present
part. Curved-Dunn $H^2$ vanishing at higher genus is a conditional
transport theorem: modular-bootstrap acyclicity plus the
degree-$2$ comparison imply the curved-Dunn conclusion
(\cref{thm:curved-dunn-H2-vanishing-all-genera}). Irregular-singular
KZB regularity is a separate boundary-composition theorem under
the boundary formal-type hypotheses
(\cref{thm:irregular-singular-kzb-regularity}). The
class-$\mathbf{M}$ chain-level DS--Hochschild compatibility closes
only after the completed Drinfeld--Sokolov bar-coalgebra SDR,
the induced coderivation transfer, and the bounded-shift HPL
convergence package are supplied
(\cref{thm:chd-ds-hochschild} +
\cref{cor:universal-holography-class-M}); the periodic-CDG
admissible-level closure closes via Arkhipov--Gaitsgory twisted
Satake + Finkelberg tilting semisimplification (Vol~I); and the
chiral Deligne--Tamarkin chain-level associator-dependence closes
after fixing a Drinfeld associator
(\cref{thm:chd-deligne-tamarkin}). Two
Vol~II-native chapters supply the closing surface:
the Koszulness Moduli scheme
$M_{\mathrm{Kosz}}$ with fourteen-chart atlas and
$\mathrm{GRT}_1(\Q)$-torsor action
(\cref{chap:koszulness-moduli}), and the Infinite Fingerprint
Classification with six-slot $\varphi'$ and $\mathbf{G/L/C/M/FF}$
projection (\cref{chap:infinite-fingerprint}). The four
irreducible fronts close on their proved loci: class-$\mathbf{M}$
in the weight-completed / pro / $J$-adic ambient unless finite
propagation supplies the raw direct-sum complex; periodic-CDG on
the verified admissible exact locus. The residual three directions
F1$'$, F4$'$, F6 are boundary faces: the
logarithmic-to-non-logarithmic transition (F1$'$), the
bosonic-to-super-Yangian canonicalisation of the complementarity
pairing (F4$'$), and the Faber socle-evaluation residue at $g \ge 3$
	(F6); these are the logarithmic, super-Yangian, and Faber-residue
	boundary faces of the Vol~I obstruction tower. The operator-level
	open objects are the
four Construction Problems CP1--CP4 inscribed at
\cref{rem:four-construction-problems} (closing remark of
Part~\ref{part:gravity}); their stage-stratification across the
universal stage chain is
\cref{thm:construction-problem-stage-stratification}.
